Normalize p to sum=1. Use log p (since p>0). We can initialize logp = log(p) - log(S). Objective with logsumexp:
```
def logsumexp_q(p, beta):
    q = conv_fft(p,p)[:2n-1]
    # q nonnegative sum=1, small values.
    x = beta*q
    m = max(x)
    return m + log(sum(exp(x-m)))
```

```

```

        # compute p from z? If z changed, p = softmax(z); but z from previous p. We can update z then p.

        scaled = beta * H

```

C. Optimization function `multiplicative_balance(p, n, budget, eta, tau, beta?, active_power, center_method)`:
We can operate unnormalized. Score computed as `2*n / (S^2 / M?)` Actually `score = 2*n / S^2` if M=max(q)=1. General: `score=2*n*max(q)/S^2`. We can scale p so M=1 after each update, but keep best unscaled shape.

Update approaches:
1. Active average gradient: Given p, q. Let M=max(q). Set active weights w_k = ((q_k - tau*M)/M)^γ for q_k>tau*M, else 0. Or softmax `w=exp(β(q-M))` with q normalized by M. Since M=1 after scaling, q in [0,1]. Use `w = (q - tau)**power` clipped. We want include constraints near max. For tau=0.5, power=1.
2. Compute G_i = ∑_{k} w_k p_{k-i} via FFT (same H). This is the gradient of ∑ w_k q_k divided by 2. To reduce active q while maintaining sum? We want move p to low G. Update `logp -= eta * (G - c)`, where c can be weighted mean `np.dot(p, G)/S`? If we subtract weighted mean, the sum of log changes weighted by p zero: ∑ p_i (G_i - mean) =0, so p sum unchanged to first order; normalization minor. This is a natural balance direction (reduces high G relative). Eta small.
After update, p may become zeros; clip to tiny. Then scale to M=1. Evaluate score. If score improved, accept; else maybe revert or adjust eta.

Let's define helper functions:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    r=1
    while r<m: r*=2
    return r

def fft_self_conv(p, n, rfft_cache?):
    L=2*n-1
    R=next_pow2(L)
    f=np.fft.rfft(p, R)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f, R)[:L]
    return np.maximum(c,0) # clip tiny negative

def fft_contribution(p, w, n):
    # H_i = sum_j w_{i+j} p_j for i=0..n-1
    L=2*n-1
    R=next_pow2(n+L-1)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,R)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],R) # w reversed length L
    D=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw,R)
    start=2*n-2
    H=D[start-1 : start-n : -1]? Need correct indices.
```
Let's recalc H formula: D=conv(p, w[::-1]) length n+L-1 =3n-3. D[s]=∑_{j=0}^{n-1} p_j wr[L-1 + s - j]? wr[k]=w[L-1-k]. We found H_i = D[L-1 - i] with L-1=2n-2. So indices s=2n-2 - i for i=0..n-1: from 2n-2 down to n-1. Python: `H = D[2*n-2 : n-2 : -1]` (stop exclusive n-2). For n=3, start=4, stop=1 => D[4],D[3],D[2]. Good. For n=1? start=0, stop=-1? Sequence length n=1: D conv p length1 w length1 -> D[0]. We need H0=D0. `D[0 : -1 : -1]` would be empty? Need handle n=1. But we use n>1. Still robust:
```
if n==1: return D[0:1]
H = D[2*n-2 : n-2 : -1]
```
For n=2 start=2 stop=0 -> D[2],D[1]. Check p len2, L=3, D len4; H0=D2, H1=D1 good.

But D from FFT may have imaginary; use real. Also due to floating, H might negative tiny; clip 0.

Scoring via FFT:
```
def score_fft(p,n):
    S=p.sum()
    if S<=0: return inf
    q=fft_self_conv(p,n)
    M=q.max()
    return 2*n*M/(S*S)
```

We can compute exact with `np.convolve(p,p)` for candidate list. Use exact for best recording every e.g. 1000 iterations or phase end.

```
def initial_candidates(n):
    base = central_binom(n)
    base_norm = base/base.sum()
    cands=[base_norm]
    # reversed
    cands.append(base_norm[::-1])
    # endpoint boosted: base * exp(a*t) for a in [-2,-1,0,1,2], and exp(a*(t-0.5))
    t = np.linspace(0,1,n-1)
    for a in [-3,-2,-1,0,1,2,3]:
       p=base*np.exp(a*t)
       p/=p.sum(); cands.append(p)
    for b in [-2,-1,0,1,2]:
       p=base*np.exp(b*(t-0.5)**2); ...
    # symmetric mixture of base and reverse: (1-m)base + m reverse for m in [0.1,0.25,0.5]
    # random smooth: base * exp(sum r_k sin(2πk t+phase)) for k=1,2,3 amplitudes small.
```

```
import numpy as np, time, math

def _next_pow2(m): ...
def _central_binom(n): recurrence
def _score_fft(p,n,R=None): returns score, q maybe
def _conv_fft(p,n,R): ...
def _contrib_fft(p,w,n,R): ...
def _softmax_z(z): ...
def _exact_score(p): try evaluate_sequence
def _clip_nonneg(p): ...

def _balance_pass(p, n, R, seconds, weight_mode='softmax', beta_start=..., beta_end=..., tau_start=..., tau_end=..., target_change_start=0.15, target_change_end=0.01, momentum=0.5, exact_cooldown=1.0, exact_limit=50):
    z = log(p)
    best_p = p.copy(); best_fft = score; best_exact = inf
    last_exact = time.monotonic(); exact_count=0
    start_time = time.monotonic(); deadline = start_time + seconds
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       progress=(now-start)/seconds
       if weight_mode=='softmax':
          beta = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*progress
       ...
       p = softmax_z(z)
       q=conv...
       qmax=q.max()
       score_fft=2*n*qmax  # sum=1
       if score_fft < best_fft: best_fft=score_fft; best_p=p.copy(); maybe exact if cooldown
       if qmax<=0: break
       # weights
       if mode softmax: x=beta*(q-qmax); x; w=exp; wsum; w/=wsum
       elif active: w=np.maximum(q - tau*qmax,0); if sum==0 w[np.argmax(q)]=1; else /=sum
       elif mixed: w= (q/qmax)**power? 
       H=contrib...
       mean=np.dot(p,H)
       d=H-mean
       scale = np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d)+1e-300)
       if scale < 1e-18: break or add noise
       d = d/scale
       d = d - np.dot(p,d)
       pd = p*d
       mx = np.max(np.abs(pd))
       if mx < 1e-30: break
       delta = pd/mx * target_change
       if momentum: mom_delta = momentum*mom_delta + (1-momentum)*delta
       z = z - mom_delta
       # clip z
       z = np.clip(z, -700, 700)
       # If many p underflow? no.
       # maybe if no improvement for long perturb
    # final exact
```

Let's refine mathematical initial base. Central binom recurrence:
```
def central_base(n):
    p=np.empty(n)
    p[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
        p[i]=p[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
    return p
```
Check p_i = binom(2i,i)/4^i. For i=1 0.5; yes. p values all <=1, sum O√n. Good.

Now, candidate generation exact/FFT. Let's code `_fft_score` efficiently:
```
def _fft_score(p,n,R=None):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    S=p.sum()
    if S<=0: return inf
    p=p/S
    if R is None: R=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    f=np.fft.rfft(p,R)
    q=np.fft.irfft(f*f,R)[:2*n-1]
    q=np.maximum(q,0)
    return float(2*n*q.max())
```

       # optional if not improvement many, reduce sigma?

Detailed code pieces:

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    r=1
    while r < m:
        r <<= 1
    return r
```

```
def _normalize_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).copy()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    p /= s
    return p
```

`_central_base(n)`:
```
p=np.ones(n,float)
for i in range(1,n): p[i]=p[i-1]*((2*i-1)/(2*i))
return p
```
For large n, p_i ~1/sqrt(pi i), no underflow for n=4806. Good.

`_fft_score` exact for normalized p:
```
def _fft_score(p,n,R=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return inf
    p = p/s
    L=2*n-1
    if R is None: R=_next_pow2(L)
    f=np.fft.rfft(p,R)
    q=np.fft.irfft(f*f,R)[:L]
    np.maximum(q,0,out=q)
    return float(2.0*n*q.max())
```
Using `np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)` okay? `np.maximum` supports out. If q small negative, set 0.

`_contrib_fft(p,w,n,R)`:
```
def _contrib_fft(p,w,n,R):
    L=2*n-1
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,R)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],R)
    d=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw,R).real
    if n==1:
        return d[0:1]
    return d[2*n-2 : n-2 : -1]
```
But note `fp` already available in balance; to avoid recompute, we can separate but fine. For speed, in pass we compute fp once and call a helper `_contrib_from_fp(fp,w,n,R)`. Let's implement:
```
def _contrib_from_fp(fp,w,n,R):
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],R)
    d=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw,R).real
    if n==1: return d[0:1]
    return d[2*n-2 : n-2 : -1]
```
And q from fp:
```
def _q_from_fp(fp,n,R):
    q=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,R)[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(q,0,out=q)
    return q
```
In pass, compute `fp=np.fft.rfft(p,R)`; q=_q_from_fp; H=_contrib_from_fp.

Need ensure R large enough for `fp*fw`: p len n, w len 2n-1 -> product length 3n-3; R chosen. For q, p*p length 2n-1 <=R.

def _weights_from_q(q,qmax,mode,focus,tau):
    if mode == 'active' or mode == 'power':
       if mode=='active':
          w=np.maximum(q - tau*qmax, 0.0)
       else:
          ratio=np.maximum(q/qmax, 0.0)
          w=ratio**? # power? Actually ratio^power makes low q small; high ratio 1. Use w = np.power(np.clip(ratio,0,1), power)
       ws=w.sum()
       if ws <= 0 or not finite: w=np.zeros_like(q); w[int(np.argmax(q))]=1.0
       else: w/=ws
    else: # softmax focus
       ratio=q/qmax if qmax>0 else zeros
       x=focus*(ratio - 1.0)
       # focus maybe; x <=0 (max -0, min -inf). Add -? stable
       w=np.exp(x)
       ws=w.sum()
       if ws...
       w/=ws
    return w
```

Progress `prog = min(1.0, (now-start)/max(seconds,1e-9))`.
`focus = focus_start + (focus_end - focus_start)*prog`
`tau = tau_start + (tau_end - tau_start)*prog`
`change = change_start + (change_end - change_start)*prog`
`mom = momentum`

`_softmax_z`:
```
def _softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z,float)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -700.0)
    zm = z.max()
    e = np.exp(z-zm)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(len(z))/len(z)
    return e/s
```

```
def _exact_score_from_array(p):
    try:
        seq = [float(x) for x in p]
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')

Now, code details for time allocation:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except: budget=2400
    final_reserve = 45.0
    deadline = start + max(1.0, budget - final_reserve)
    # determine n
    n = 4806

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _docstring? (module docstring)

At top, include docstring.

Function `_by_score(item): return item[0]`.

`_next_pow2`:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    r=1
    while r < int(max(1,m)):
        r <<= 1
    return r
```

`_clamp_p`:
```
def _clean_p(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
    if p.ndim !=1: p=p.ravel()
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    return p
```

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize_p(p):
    p=_clean_p(p)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <=1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    p = p / s
    return p
```

`_central_base` handles n.

`_fft_score` as above.

`_q_from_fp`, `_contrib_from_fp`.

`_softmax_z`:
```
def _softmax_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float)
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -700.0)
    zm=z.max()
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        return np.ones(len(z), dtype=float)/len(z)
    e=np.exp(z-zm)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(len(z), dtype=float)/len(z)
    return e/s
```

`_exact_score_from_array` as above.

`_initial_candidates`:
```
def _initial_candidates(n, extra):
    cands=[]
    base=_central_base(n)
    p=_normalize_p(base)
    if p is not None: cands.append(p)
    cands.append(p[::-1].copy())
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n-1) if n>1 else np.array([0.0])
    lin = [-3,-2,-1.5,-1,-0.75,-0.5,-0.25,0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1,1.5,2,3]
    for a in lin:
       q=_normalize_p(base*np.exp(a*(t-0.5)))
       if q is not None: cands.append(q)
    quad=[-1.5,-1,-0.5,0,0.5,1,1.5]
    for b in quad: ...
    for a in [-1,-0.5,0,0.5,1]:
      for b in [-1,-0.5,0,0.5,1]:
         q=_normalize_p(base*np.exp(a*(t-0.5)+b*(t-0.5)**2))
         ...
    rng=np.random.default_rng(777)
    for _ in range(40):
       f = rng.normal(0,0.5)*(t-0.5) + rng.normal(0,0.5)*(t-0.5)**2 + rng.normal(0,0.3)*np.sin(np.pi*t) + rng.normal(0,0.3)*np.sin(2*np.pi*t)
       q=_normalize_p(base*np.exp(f))
    if extra is not None:
       arr=np.asarray(extra,dtype=float)
       if arr.ndim==1 and arr.size==n:
          q=_normalize_p(arr)
          if q is not None: cands.append(q)
    # filter finite
    good=[]
    for p in cands:
       if p is not None and p.size==n and np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and p.sum()>0:
           good.append(p)
    if not good:
       good.append(_central_base(n)/np.sum(_central_base(n)))
    return good
```

Now, `_balance_pass`:
```
def _balance_pass(p_in,n,R,seconds,mode='softmax',focus_start=20.0,focus_end=0.5,tau_start=0.9,tau_end=0.2,change_start=0.05,change_end=0.001,momentum=0.4,seed=1,max_no_improve=20.0):
    if seconds <=0: return p_in, _fft_score(p_in,n,R)
    p0=_normalize_p(p_in)
    if p0 is None: p0=_central_base(n)/sum
    z=np.log(p0+1e-300)
    best_p=p0.copy()
    best_fft=_fft_score(p0,n,R)
    z_best=z.copy()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(0.0,seconds)
    mom_delta=np.zeros(n)
    last_improve=start
    restarts=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= deadline: break
       prog=(now-start)/max(1e-9, seconds)
       if prog >1: prog=1
       if mode == 'active':
           tau = tau_start + (tau_end-tau_start)*prog
           focus = 0.0
       else:
           focus = focus_start + (focus_end-focus_start)*prog
           tau = 0.0
       change = change_start + (change_end-change_start)*prog
       p=_softmax_z(z)
       fp=np.fft.rfft(p,R)
       q=_q_from_fp(fp,n,R)
       qmax=float(q.max())
       if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <=0:
           break
       score=float(2.0*n*qmax)
       if score < best_fft - 1e-12:
           best_fft=score
           best_p=p.copy()
           z_best=z.copy()
           last_improve=now
       if now - last_improve > max_no_improve and restarts < 30:
           z = z_best + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
           mom_delta[:] = 0.0
           restarts += 1
           last_improve = now
           continue  # next iteration after restart
       # weights
       if mode == 'active':
           w=np.maximum(q - tau*qmax, 0.0)
           ws=float(w.sum())
           if ws <=1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
               w=np.zeros_like(q); w[int(np.argmax(q))]=1.0; ws=1.0
           w /= ws
       else:
           if qmax > 1e-300:
               ratio = q / qmax
           else:
               ratio = np.zeros_like(q)
           # ratio values maybe >1 due to rounding; clip to 1
           ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.0, 1.0)
           x = focus * (ratio - 1.0)
           # for very negative x, exp 0; safe
           w = np.exp(x)
           ws = float(w.sum())
           if ws <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
               w = np.zeros_like(q); w[int(np.argmax(q))]=1.0; ws=1.0
           w /= ws
       H=_contrib_from_fp(fp,w,n,R)
       H=np.maximum(H,0.0)
       mean=float(np.dot(p,H))
       d=H-mean
       scale=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d)))
       if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-18:
           z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
           mom_delta[:] = 0.0
           continue
       d = d / scale
       wmean=float(np.dot(p,d))
       d = d - wmean
       pd = p*d
       mx=float(np.max(np.abs(pd)))
       if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 1e-18:
           z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
           mom_delta[:] = 0.0
           continue
       pd = pd / mx
       mom_delta = momentum*mom_delta + (1.0-momentum)*pd
       mxm=float(np.max(np.abs(mom_delta)))
       if mxm <= 1e-18:
           mom_delta[:] = 0.0
           continue
       z = z - (change / mxm) * mom_delta
       np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0, out=z)
       it += 1
    # If best_fft finite
    return best_p, best_fft
```
One issue: If we restart and continue, we skip Q/weights for that iteration, fine. `max_no_improve` default 20s. We pass maybe 10.

For active mode, if tau<1 and q first n equal, w sum active. If no active due to tau > max ratio? max ratio 1, qmax*tau <= qmax so maximum lag active at least. Good.

`_perturb_pass`:
```
def _perturb_pass(p_in,n,R,seconds,seed=2):
   p0=_normalize...
   best=p0; best_fft=score
   z=np.log(best+1e-300)
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   start=... deadline=...
   stagnation=0
   while time<deadline:
      if rng.random()<0.2:
         sigma=rng.uniform(0.01,0.3)
         zc=z+rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
      else:
         width=int(rng.integers(max(3, n//100), max(4, n//10)))
         if width>=n: width=n
         start_idx=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
         sigma=rng.uniform(0.01,0.5)
         zc=z.copy()
         zc[start_idx:start_idx+width] += rng.normal(0,sigma,width)
      zc=np.clip(zc,-700,700)
      pc=_softmax_z(zc)
      sc=_fft_score(pc,n,R)
      if sc < best_fft - 1e-12:
          z=zc; best=pc.copy(); best_fft=sc; stagnation=0
      else:
          stagnation += 1
          if stagnation > 2000:
             # revert to best and small noise
             z = np.log(best+1e-300) + rng.normal(0,0.01,n)
             z=np.clip(z,-700,700)
             stagnation=0
   return best,best_fft
```
This could be slow if many full copies, but time small.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """..."""
    start=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except: budget=2400.0

    deadline=start+max(1.0,budget-final_reserve)

    if isinstance(hs, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
        extra=hs

        if arr.ndim==1 and 512 <= arr.size <= 10000:

       base=_central_base(n); p=base/base.sum(); sc=_fft_score(p,n,Rscore); scored=[(sc,p)]

    stored=[]

        stored.append((s4,p4))
    # maybe one final softmax small

```python
"""
Minimizes the evaluator for nonnegative step-function heights.

The objective is scale invariant.  With sum(p)=1 it is
    score = 2*n*max(p*p),
where p*p is the full linear convolution.

A strong analytic starting point is the first n coefficients of
(1-x)^(-1/2), whose square has flat initial coefficients.  The program
starts from several endpoint-adjusted versions of this construction and from
any supplied height_sequence_1.  It then runs an adaptive smooth-minimax
mirror-descent "balancing" pass:

  q = p*p
  w = softmax(focus*(q/max(q)-1)) or hard active-set weights
  G_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i}
  z -= eta * p_i * (G_i - E_p[G])

which down-weights coefficients contributing to large convolution lags and
up-weights coefficients contributing to slack lags.  A short final perturbative
local search is included.  FFTs are used for all internal evaluations; the
returned sequence is selected by calling the official evaluator on the best
FFT-score candidates.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _by_score(item):
    return item[0]


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    r = 1
    while r < m:
        r <<= 1
    return r


def _clean_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if p.ndim != 1:
        p = p.ravel()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    return p


def _normalize_p(p):
    p = _clean_p(p)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    p = p / s
    return p


def _central_base(n):
    """First n coefficients of (1-x)^(-1/2), normalized by summation later."""
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(1, n):
        p[i] = p[i - 1] * ((2 * i - 1) / (2 * i))
    return p


def _fft_score(p, n, rfft_len=None):
    p = _normalize_p(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if rfft_len is None:
        rfft_len = _next_pow2(L)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, rfft_len)
    q = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, rfft_len)[:L]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    qmax = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * qmax)


def _q_from_fp(fp, n, rfft_len):
    q = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, rfft_len)[:2 * n - 1]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    return q


def _contrib_from_fp(fp, w, n, rfft_len):
    # Returns H_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i} for i=0..n-1.
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], rfft_len)
    d = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, rfft_len).real
    if n == 1:
        return d[0:1]
    return d[2 * n - 2 : n - 2 : -1]


def _softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -700.0)
    zm = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        return np.ones(len(z), dtype=float) / float(len(z))
    e = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(len(z), dtype=float) / float(len(z))
    return e / s


def _exact_score_from_array(p):
    try:
        seq = [float(x) for x in p]
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _initial_candidates(n, extra_seq):
    cands = []

    if n <= 1:
        return [np.array([1.0], dtype=float)]

    base = _central_base(n)
    p0 = _normalize_p(base)
    if p0 is not None:
        cands.append(p0)
        cands.append(p0[::-1].copy())

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=float)
    xm = t - 0.5

    # Endpoint-boosted variants of the central-binomial shape.
    for a in (-3.0, -2.0, -1.5, -1.0, -0.75, -0.5, -0.35,
              0.0, 0.35, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0):
        q = _normalize_p(base * np.exp(a * xm))
        if q is not None:
            cands.append(q)

    for b in (-2.0, -1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        q = _normalize_p(base * np.exp(b * xm * xm))
        if q is not None:
            cands.append(q)

    for a in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
        for b in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
            q = _normalize_p(base * np.exp(a * xm + b * xm * xm))
            if q is not None:
                cands.append(q)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(777)
    for _ in range(48):
        f = (
            rng.normal(0.0, 0.55) * xm
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.45) * xm * xm
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.30) * np.sin(np.pi * t)
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t)
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.15) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
        )
        q = _normalize_p(base * np.exp(f))
        if q is not None:
            cands.append(q)

    if extra_seq is not None:
        arr = np.asarray(extra_seq, dtype=float)
        if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size == n:
            q = _normalize_p(arr)
            if q is not None:
                cands.append(q)

    good = []
    for p in cands:
        if p is None or p.size != n:
            continue
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            continue
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        good.append(p / s)

    if not good:
        base = _central_base(n)
        good.append(base / float(base.sum()))

    return good


def _balance_pass(
    p_in,
    n,
    rfft_len,
    seconds,
    mode="softmax",
    focus_start=25.0,
    focus_end=0.5,
    tau_start=0.90,
    tau_end=0.20,
    change_start=0.05,
    change_end=0.001,
    momentum=0.4,
    seed=1,
    max_no_improve=15.0,
):
    p0 = _normalize_p(p_in)
    if p0 is None:
        p0 = _central_base(n)
        p0 = p0 / float(p0.sum())

    z = np.log(p0 + 1e-300)
    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_fft = _fft_score(p0, n, rfft_len)
    z_best = z.copy()

    if seconds <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_fft

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(seconds))
    mom_delta = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    last_improve = start
    restarts = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break

        prog = (now - start) / max(1e-9, float(seconds))
        if prog < 0.0:
            prog = 0.0
        if prog > 1.0:
            prog = 1.0

        if mode == "active":
            tau = tau_start + (tau_end - tau_start) * prog
            focus = 0.0
        else:
            focus = focus_start + (focus_end - focus_start) * prog
            tau = 0.0

        change = change_start + (change_end - change_start) * prog

        if now - last_improve > max_no_improve and restarts < 30:
            z = z_best + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
            mom_delta[:] = 0.0
            restarts += 1
            last_improve = now
            continue

        p = _softmax_z(z)
        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, rfft_len)
        q = _q_from_fp(fp, n, rfft_len)
        qmax = float(q.max())

        if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
            break

        score = float(2.0 * n * qmax)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_fft - 1e-12:
            best_fft = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            z_best = z.copy()
            last_improve = now

        # Weight active / high convolution lags.
        if mode == "active":
            w = np.maximum(q - tau * qmax, 0.0)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 1e-300:
                w = np.zeros_like(q)
                w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
                ws = 1.0
            w /= ws
        else:
            ratio = q / qmax
            ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.0, 1.0)
            x = focus * (ratio - 1.0)
            w = np.exp(x)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 1e-300:
                w = np.zeros_like(q)
                w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
                ws = 1.0
            w /= ws

        h = _contrib_from_fp(fp, w, n, rfft_len)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)

        mean_h = float(np.dot(p, h))
        d = h - mean_h
        scale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))

        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-18:
            z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
            mom_delta[:] = 0.0
            continue

        d = d / scale
        d = d - float(np.dot(p, d))

        pd = p * d
        mx = float(np.max(np.abs(pd)))
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 1e-18:
            z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
            mom_delta[:] = 0.0
            continue

        pd /= mx
        mom_delta = momentum * mom_delta + (1.0 - momentum) * pd
        mxm = float(np.max(np.abs(mom_delta)))
        if not np.isfinite(mxm) or mxm <= 1e-18:
            mom_delta[:] = 0.0
            continue

        z = z - (change / mxm) * mom_delta
        np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0, out=z)

    return best_p, best_fft


def _perturb_pass(p_in, n, rfft_len, seconds, seed=2):
    p0 = _normalize_p(p_in)
    if p0 is None:
        p0 = _central_base(n)
        p0 = p0 / float(p0.sum())

    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_fft = _fft_score(p0, n, rfft_len)
    z = np.log(best_p + 1e-300)

    if seconds <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_fft

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(seconds))
    non_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        if rng.random() < 0.18:
            sigma = rng.uniform(0.008, 0.22)
            zc = z + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
        else:
            lo = max(3, n // 120)
            hi = max(lo + 1, n // 12)
            width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
            if width >= n:
                width = n
            start_idx = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
            sigma = rng.uniform(0.008, 0.45)
            zc = z.copy()
            zc[start_idx:start_idx + width] += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, width)

        np.clip(zc, -700.0, 700.0, out=zc)
        pc = _softmax_z(zc)
        sc = _fft_score(pc, n, rfft_len)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_fft - 1e-12:
            best_fft = sc
            best_p = pc.copy()
            z = zc.copy()
            non_improve = 0
        else:
            non_improve += 1
            if non_improve > 3000:
                z = np.log(best_p + 1e-300) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.012, n)
                np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0, out=z)
                non_improve = 0

    return best_p, best_fft


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a low-score nonnegative sequence and return it as a Python list.
    The time budget is used adaptively for FFT-based balancing and local search.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = max(90.0, budget)

    final_reserve = 50.0
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - final_reserve)

    # Choose length.  Prefer supplied length when sane; otherwise 4806.
    n = 4806
    extra_seq = None
    hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if isinstance(hs, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
        extra_seq = hs
        arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=float)
        if arr.ndim == 1 and 512 <= arr.size <= 10000:
            n = int(arr.size)

    if n < 2:
        return [1.0]

    rfft_len = _next_pow2(3 * n - 3)
    rfft_score = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    print(f"[propose] using n={n}, rfft_len={rfft_len}, budget={budget:.1f}s")

    candidates = _initial_candidates(n, extra_seq)
    scored = []
    for p in candidates:
        sc = _fft_score(p, n, rfft_score)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, p))

    if not scored:
        base = _central_base(n)
        p = base / float(base.sum())
        scored.append((_fft_score(p, n, rfft_score), p))

    scored.sort(key=_by_score)
    print(f"[propose] initial best FFT score={scored[0][0]:.10f}")

    stored = []
    for sc, p in scored[:10]:
        stored.append((sc, p))

    total = max(1.0, deadline - t0)

    # Main global balancing on the best initial shape.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        secs = min(0.44 * total, deadline - time.monotonic())
        p1, s1 = _balance_pass(
            scored[0][1],
            n,
            rfft_len,
            max(0.0, secs),
            mode="softmax",
            focus_start=25.0,
            focus_end=0.5,
            change_start=0.050,
            change_end=0.001,
            momentum=0.40,
            seed=101,
            max_no_improve=15.0,
        )
        stored.append((s1, p1))
        print(f"[propose] softmax pass 1 FFT score={s1:.10f}")

    # Active-set polishing on the current best.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        stored.sort(key=_by_score)
        pbest = stored[0][1]
        secs = min(0.20 * total, deadline - time.monotonic())
        p2, s2 = _balance_pass(
            pbest,
            n,
            rfft_len,
            max(0.0, secs),
            mode="active",
            tau_start=0.90,
            tau_end=0.20,
            change_start=0.040,
            change_end=0.001,
            momentum=0.50,
            seed=202,
            max_no_improve=12.0,
        )
        stored.append((s2, p2))
        print(f"[propose] active pass FFT score={s2:.10f}")

    # Refine a second distinct starting point to avoid one local basin.
    if len(scored) > 1 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        secs = min(0.14 * total, deadline - time.monotonic())
        p3, s3 = _balance_pass(
            scored[1][1],
            n,
            rfft_len,
            max(0.0, secs),
            mode="softmax",
            focus_start=18.0,
            focus_end=0.3,
            change_start=0.040,
            change_end=0.001,
            momentum=0.40,
            seed=303,
            max_no_improve=12.0,
        )
        stored.append((s3, p3))
        print(f"[propose] second start softmax FFT score={s3:.10f}")

    # Refine a third distinct starting point.
    if len(scored) > 2 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        secs = min(0.10 * total, deadline - time.monotonic())
        p4, s4 = _balance_pass(
            scored[2][1],
            n,
            rfft_len,
            max(0.0, secs),
            mode="active",
            tau_start=0.85,
            tau_end=0.30,
            change_start=0.040,
            change_end=0.001,
            momentum=0.50,
            seed=404,
            max_no_improve=12.0,
        )
        stored.append((s4, p4))
        print(f"[propose] third start active FFT score={s4:.10f}")

    # Small soft polishing pass on the current best.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        stored.sort(key=_by_score)
        secs = min(0.07 * total, deadline - time.monotonic())
        p5, s5 = _balance_pass(
            stored[0][1],
            n,
            rfft_len,
            max(0.0, secs),
            mode="softmax",
            focus_start=3.0,
            focus_end=0.1,
            change_start=0.014,
            change_end=0.0004,
            momentum=0.30,
            seed=505,
            max_no_improve=10.0,
        )
        stored.append((s5, p5))
        print(f"[propose] final soft pass FFT score={s5:.10f}")

    # Block/local random logit perturbations on the current best.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        stored.sort(key=_by_score)
        secs = min(0.05 * total, deadline - time.monotonic())
        p6, s6 = _perturb_pass(
            stored[0][1],
            n,
            rfft_len,
            max(0.0, secs),
            seed=606,
        )
        stored.append((s6, p6))
        print(f"[propose] local perturb FFT score={s6:.10f}")

    # Select by official evaluator on the best FFT candidates.
    stored.sort(key=_by_score)
    max_exact = 8 if n > 6000 else 14
    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_seq = None

    for i in range(min(max_exact, len(stored))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline + max(1.0, 0.03 * final_reserve):
            break
        sc, p = stored[i]
        ex = _exact_score_from_array(p)
        print(f"[propose] candidate {i}: fft={sc:.10f} exact={ex:.10f}")
        if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact:
            best_exact = ex
            best_seq = p

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = stored[0][1]

    best_seq = _clean_p(best_seq)
    s = float(best_seq.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        base = _central_base(n)
        best_seq = base / float(base.sum())
    else:
        best_seq = best_seq / s

    # Final Python normalization for the official evaluator.
    seq = [float(x) for x in best_seq]
    py_sum = 0.0
    for x in seq:
        if math.isfinite(x) and x > 0.0:
            py_sum += x
    if py_sum > 0.0:
        seq = [x / py_sum for x in seq]

    clean = []
    for x in seq:
        if not math.isfinite(x) or x < 0.0:
            clean.append(0.0)
        else:
            clean.append(min(1000.0, x))

    final_sum = 0.0
    for x in clean:
        final_sum += x
    if final_sum <= 0.01:
        clean = [1.0 for _ in range(n)]

    if np.isfinite(best_exact):
        print(f"[propose] final selected exact score={best_exact:.10f}, length={len(clean)}")
    else:
        print(f"[propose] final selected FFT score={stored[0][0]:.10f}, length={len(clean)}")

    return clean
```
